  388:             return Optional.empty();
  389:         }
  390:         if (!walletExecuted) {
  391:             log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but wallet controls declined execution; no paper position was created.", executionSignal.getId());
  392:             return Optional.empty();
  393:         }
  394: 
  395:         BigDecimal executedQuantity = walletTradeRepository
  396:                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(executionSignal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
  397:                 .map(com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletTrade::getQuantity)
  398:                 .filter(q -> q != null && q.signum() > 0)
  399:                 .orElse(riskModelQuantity);
  400: 
  401:         executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, executionDecision);
  402:         PaperPosition position = positionRepository.save(PaperPosition.builder()
  403:                 .symbol(symbol)
  404:                 .side(PositionSide.BUY)
  405:                 .status(PositionStatus.OPEN)
  406:                 .quantity(executedQuantity)
  407:                 .entryPrice(freshExecutionPrice.price())
  408:                 .stopLoss(executionSignal.getStopLoss())
  409:                 .takeProfit(executionSignal.getTakeProfit())
  410:                 .signal(executionSignal)
  411:                 .entryReason(executionSignal.getExplanation() + " | Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "]: " + executionDecision.explanation() + " Effective position " + effectivePositionPercent + "%.")
  412:                 .openedAt(Instant.now())
  413:                 .build());
  414:         return Optional.of(position);
  415:     }

  423:     @Transactional
  424:     public PaperPosition close(Long positionId, BigDecimal exitPrice) {
  425:         PaperPosition position = positionRepository.findById(positionId)
  426:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Position not found"));
  427: 
  428:         if (position.getStatus() != PositionStatus.OPEN) {
  429:             throw new IllegalStateException("Position is already closed");
  430:         }
  431: 
  432:         return completeClose(position, exitPrice,
  433:                 exitPrice.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0 ? PositionStatus.STOPPED : PositionStatus.CLOSED,
  434:                 "MANUAL_CLOSE", "Position was manually closed.", null);
  435:     }
  436: 
  437:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  438:     public List<PaperPosition> list() {
  439:         return positionRepository.findTop100ByOrderByOpenedAtDesc();
  440:     }

  443:             PaperPosition position,

  445:             PositionStatus status,
  446:             String closeReason,
  447:             String explanation
  448:     ) {
  449:         return completeClose(position, signal.getLatestPrice(), status, closeReason, explanation, signal);
  450:     }

  460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.

 1336:     private ExecutionDecision exceptionalStrengthProbeDecision(
 1337:             TradeSignal current, Evidence e, HardRiskBlock block) {
 1338:         if (current == null || block == null || !block.blocked()) return null;
 1339:         if (!"BTC_CONTEXT_BLOCKED".equals(block.code())) return null;
 1340: 
 1341:         // BTC is allowed to reduce exposure here, not to manufacture extra score.
 1342:         // Every other risk authority remains mandatory.
 1343:         if (!current.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
 1344:                 || !current.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()
 1345:                 || !current.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
 1346:                 || !current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1347:         if (current.getLatestPrice() == null || current.getStopLoss() == null || current.getTakeProfit() == null
 1348:                 || current.getStopLoss().signum() <= 0 || current.getTakeProfit().signum() <= 0) return null;
 1349: 
 1350:         if (current.getOriginalDecision() != SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) return null;
 1351:         if (current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.WATCH && !isBullish(current.getDecision())) return null;
 1352:         if (current.getTotalScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_SCORE
 1353:                 || current.getTrendScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_TREND
 1354:                 || current.getMomentumScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_MOMENTUM
 1355:                 || current.getVolumeScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_VOLUME) return null;
 1356: 
 1357:         // This route is specifically a reversal probe, not a generic high-score bypass.
 1358:         // Require a fresh bearish -> exceptional bullish acceleration on the same 1m stream.
 1359:         TradeSignal prior = recentBearishReversalAnchor(current);
 1360:         if (prior == null) return null;
 1361:         SignalDecision priorEffective = strongerDecision(prior.getDecision(), prior.getOriginalDecision());
 1362:         int scoreJump = current.getTotalScore() - prior.getTotalScore();
 1363:         if (scoreJump < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_SCORE_JUMP) return null;
 1364: 
 1365:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1366:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1367:         if (five == null || oneHour == null) return null;
 1368:         if (isBearish(five.getDecision()) || isBearish(five.getOriginalDecision())
 1369:                 || isBearish(oneHour.getDecision()) || isBearish(oneHour.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1370: 
 1371:         SignalDecision btcDecision = current.getBtcContextDecision();
 1372:         int percent = btcDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
 1373:                 ? EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_BTC_STRONG_SELL_PERCENT
 1374:                 : EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_BTC_SELL_PERCENT;
 1375: 
 1376:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1377:                 "EXCEPTIONAL_STRENGTH_PROBE",
 1378:                 "BTC_CONFLICT_REDUCED_PROBE",
 1379:                 percent,
 1380:                 "High-conviction reversal qualified for a reduced BTC-conflict probe: prior="
 1381:                         + priorEffective + " " + prior.getTotalScore() + " -> current="
 1382:                         + current.getOriginalDecision() + " " + current.getTotalScore()
 1383:                         + " (score jump +" + scoreJump + "), trend=" + current.getTrendScore()
 1384:                         + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore() + ", volume=" + current.getVolumeScore()
 1385:                         + ", 1m=" + current.getDecision() + ", 5m=" + five.getDecision()
 1386:                         + ", 1h=" + oneHour.getDecision() + ", BTC=" + (btcDecision == null ? "BEARISH" : btcDecision)
 1387:                         + ". BTC weakness reduces exposure to " + percent
 1388:                         + "% instead of increasing the technical score or bypassing asset-timeframe confirmation.",
 1389:                 e
 1390:         );
 1391:     }

 1393:     private ExecutionDecision applyExceptionalProbeEntryQualityGuard(ExecutionDecision decision, EntryQuality q) {
 1394:         if (decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return decision;
 1395:         // A probe is specifically for early reversal participation, but it must still
 1396:         // have a valid reward/risk plan and may not be an obvious late-stage chase.
 1397:         if (q.rewardRisk() <= 0d || q.score() < 40) {
 1398:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1399:                     "EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_PRICE_QUALITY_BLOCKED",
 1400:                     "Exceptional technical strength is present, but probe entry quality is only " + q.score()
 1401:                             + "/100 (" + q.classification() + "), expansion=" + format(q.expansionPercent())
 1402:                             + "%, ATR extension=" + format(q.atrExtension()) + ", R/R=" + format(q.rewardRisk()) + ".",
 1403:                     decision.evidence());
 1404:         }
 1405:         return decision;
 1406:     }

 1408:     private ExecutionDecision higherTimeframeTransitionDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1409:         if (current == null || current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1410:         if (!isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current)) return null;
 1411:         if (current.getTotalScore() < TRANSITION_MIN_CURRENT_SCORE
 1412:                 || current.getConfidenceScore() < TRANSITION_MIN_CURRENT_CONFIDENCE) return null;
 1413:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < TRANSITION_MIN_HEALTH) return null;
 1414: 
 1415:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1416:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1417:         if (five == null || oneHour == null) return null;
 1418: 
 1419:         boolean fiveSupportive = (five.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(five.getDecision()))
 1420:                 && five.getTotalScore() >= TRANSITION_MIN_5M_SCORE
 1421:                 && five.getConfidenceScore() >= TRANSITION_MIN_5M_CONFIDENCE;
 1422:         boolean strongOneHour = isBullish(oneHour.getDecision())
 1423:                 && oneHour.getTotalScore() >= TRANSITION_MIN_1H_SCORE;
 1424:         if (!fiveSupportive || !strongOneHour) return null;
 1425:         if (isBearish(current.getOriginalDecision()) || isBearish(five.getOriginalDecision())
 1426:                 || isBearish(oneHour.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1427:         if (!five.isStrategyEntryAllowed() || !five.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
 1428:                 || !five.isDerivativesEntryAllowed() || !five.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1429: 
 1430:         int percent = TRANSITION_POSITION_PERCENT;
 1431:         if (five.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() > 0) {
 1432:             percent = Math.min(percent, five.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent());
 1433:         }
 1434:         percent = Math.max(15, percent);
 1435: 
 1436:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1437:                 "HTF_TRANSITION",
 1438:                 "HTF_TRANSITION_REDUCED_ENTRY",
 1439:                 percent,
 1440:                 "Fresh higher-timeframe transition confirmed: 1h=" + oneHour.getDecision()
 1441:                         + " (score=" + oneHour.getTotalScore() + "), 5m=" + five.getDecision()
 1442:                         + " (score=" + five.getTotalScore() + ", confidence=" + five.getConfidenceScore() + ")"
 1443:                         + ", current 1m=" + current.getDecision()
 1444:                         + ", opportunity health=" + e.opportunityHealth() + "/100. "
 1445:                         + "ATR is extended, so execution is deliberately reduced to " + percent
 1446:                         + "% rather than rejecting the transition outright. Hard strategy/BTC/derivatives/liquidity vetoes remain absolute.",
 1447:                 e
 1448:         );
 1449:     }
 1450: 
 1451:     private ExecutionDecision applyTransitionEntryQualityGuard(ExecutionDecision decision, EntryQuality q) {
 1452:         if (decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return decision;
 1453:         // The transition route is specifically for a fast reversal where ATR can lag. We still
 1454:         // reject an extreme chase, but use a lower floor than the ordinary entry-quality guard.
 1455:         if (q.score() < 40 || q.rewardRisk() <= 0d) {
 1456:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1457:                     "TRANSITION_CHASE_BLOCKED",
 1458:                     "Higher-timeframe transition is valid, but price quality is too poor even for a reduced entry: "
 1459:                             + q.score() + "/100 (" + q.classification() + "), expansion="
 1460:                             + format(q.expansionPercent()) + "%, ATR extension=" + format(q.atrExtension())
 1461:                             + ", R/R=" + format(q.rewardRisk()) + ".",
 1462:                     decision.evidence());
 1463:         }
 1464:         int cap = q.score() >= 70 ? TRANSITION_POSITION_PERCENT : 20;
 1465:         int reduced = Math.min(decision.positionPercent(), cap);
 1466:         if (reduced == decision.positionPercent()) return decision;
 1467:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(decision.source(), decision.code(), reduced,
 1468:                 decision.explanation() + " Entry Quality " + q.score() + "/100 capped the transition allocation at "
 1469:                         + reduced + "%.", decision.evidence());
 1470:     }
 1471: 
 1472:     private TradeSignal setupTimeframeAtrAuthoritySignal(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1473:         if (current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1474: 
 1475:         // A neutral 1m candle is timing-neutral, not a bearish veto. A fresh 5m BUY
 1476:         // may still own setup-level ATR risk when opportunity evidence is already healthy.
 1477:         // Explicit bearish 1m information remains a veto for this fallback.
 1478:         if (isBearish(current.getDecision()) || isBearish(current.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1479:         boolean supportiveCurrent = isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current);
 1480:         boolean healthyNeutralTiming = current.getDecision() == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 1481:                 && e.opportunityHealth() >= 60
 1482:                 && e.evidenceScore() >= MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE;
 1483:         if (!supportiveCurrent && !healthyNeutralTiming) return null;
 1484: 
 1485:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1486:         if (five == null || !isBullish(five.getDecision())) return null;
 1487:         if (!five.isFinalEntryAllowed() || !five.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1488: 
 1489:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1490:         if (oneHour != null && isBearish(oneHour.getDecision())) return null;
 1491:         return five;
 1492:     }
 1493: 
 1494:     private ExecutionDecision setupTimeframeAtrAuthorityDecision(
 1495:             TradeSignal current, Evidence e, TradeSignal five) {
 1496:         int fiveRecommended = five.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() > 0
 1497:                 ? five.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent()
 1498:                 : DEFERRED_POSITION_PERCENT;
 1499:         int percent = Math.min(DEFERRED_POSITION_PERCENT, fiveRecommended);
 1500: 
 1501:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1502:                 "SETUP_TIMEFRAME_ATR",
 1503:                 "REDUCED_POSITION_ALLOWED",
 1504:                 percent,
 1505:                 "The current 1m signal is ATR-extended, but the fresh 5m BUY remains "
 1506:                         + "the setup-risk authority and its ATR plan explicitly allows immediate entry at "
 1507:                         + fiveRecommended + "%. Execution is therefore allowed at a conservative "
 1508:                         + percent + "% allocation instead of permanently rejecting the opportunity. "
 1509:                         + "A neutral 1m candle is treated as timing-neutral; explicit bearish 1m/1h context still vetoes. "
 1510:                         + "Entry Quality is evaluated with the authoritative 5m ATR so a tiny 1m ATR cannot manufacture "
 1511:                         + "a false late-stage chase classification.",
 1512:                 e
 1513:         );
 1514:     }

 1516:     private ExecutionDecision breakoutRetracementDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1517:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || current.getLatestPrice() == null) return null;
 1518:         if (isBearish(current.getDecision()) || isBearish(current.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1519:         if (!current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed() || !current.isFinalEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1520:         if (current.getTotalScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_SCORE
 1521:                 || current.getConfidenceScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_CONFIDENCE
 1522:                 || current.getTrendScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_TREND
 1523:                 || current.getMomentumScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_MOMENTUM) return null;
 1524:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_HEALTH) return null;
 1525: 
 1526:         TradeSignal origin = recentBreakoutRetracementOrigin(current);
 1527:         if (origin == null || origin.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice() == null
 1528:                 || origin.getAtrAtSignal() == null || origin.getAtrAtSignal().signum() <= 0) return null;
 1529: 
 1530:         BigDecimal target = origin.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice();
 1531:         BigDecimal atr = origin.getAtrAtSignal();
 1532:         BigDecimal upper = target.add(atr.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_TARGET_TOLERANCE_ATR)));
 1533:         BigDecimal lower = target.subtract(atr.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MAX_OVERSHOOT_ATR)));
 1534:         BigDecimal price = current.getLatestPrice();
 1535:         if (price.compareTo(upper) > 0 || price.compareTo(lower) < 0) return null;
 1536: 
 1537:         EntryQuality currentQuality = assessEntryQuality(current);
 1538:         if (currentQuality.score() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY
 1539:                 || currentQuality.rewardRisk() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_RR) return null;

 2146:     /**
 2147:      * FIX-064 / ENA #703, PEPE #756, SUI #802:
 2148:      * Derived BUY/WATCH fields can lag an exhausted or rejected price impulse. Before the three
 2149:      * affected entry routes (SCOUT, DEFERRED_CONTINUATION and ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE) open fresh
 2150:      * risk, inspect only candles that were fully CLOSED at signal.generatedAt. This deliberately
 2151:      * does not alter market-regime categories, scores, BUY thresholds or accumulated-evidence
 2152:      * semantics. Missing candle/ATR data fails open so data gaps cannot silently disable proven
 2153:      * winning routes. Production and Replay use the same timestamp-bounded repository query.
 2154:      */
 2155:     private ImmediatePriceAction immediatePriceAction(TradeSignal signal) {
 2156:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getAtrAtSignal() == null
 2157:                 || signal.getAtrAtSignal().signum() <= 0) {
 2158:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("raw candle/ATR unavailable; existing behavior preserved");
 2159:         }
 2160: 
 2161:         List<Candle> candles = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesClosedAtOrBefore(
 2162:                 signal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, signal.getGeneratedAt(),
 2163:                 PageRequest.of(0, IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_LOOKBACK_CANDLES));
 2164:         if (candles == null || candles.size() < 2) {
 2165:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("insufficient closed-candle history; existing behavior preserved");
 2166:         }
 2167: 
 2168:         Candle latest = candles.get(0);
 2169:         Candle previous = candles.get(1);
 2170:         if (latest.getOpenPrice() == null || latest.getHighPrice() == null || latest.getLowPrice() == null
 2171:                 || latest.getClosePrice() == null || previous.getClosePrice() == null) {
 2172:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("incomplete closed-candle OHLC; existing behavior preserved");
 2173:         }
 2174: 
 2175:         BigDecimal atr = signal.getAtrAtSignal();
 2176:         BigDecimal range = latest.getHighPrice().subtract(latest.getLowPrice()).abs();
 2177:         double closeLocation = range.signum() == 0 ? 0.5d
 2178:                 : latest.getClosePrice().subtract(latest.getLowPrice())
 2179:                         .divide(range, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 2180:         closeLocation = Math.max(0d, Math.min(1d, closeLocation));
 2181: 
 2182:         double bearishBodyAtr = latest.getOpenPrice().subtract(latest.getClosePrice())
 2183:                 .max(BigDecimal.ZERO).divide(atr, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 2184:         BigDecimal recentHigh = candles.stream()
 2185:                 .map(Candle::getHighPrice)
 2186:                 .filter(Objects::nonNull)
 2187:                 .max(BigDecimal::compareTo)
 2188:                 .orElse(latest.getHighPrice());
 2189:         double recentHighFadeAtr = recentHigh.subtract(latest.getClosePrice())
 2190:                 .max(BigDecimal.ZERO).divide(atr, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 2191: 
 2192:         boolean closeBelowPrevious = latest.getClosePrice().compareTo(previous.getClosePrice()) < 0;
 2193:         boolean bearishRejection = latest.getClosePrice().compareTo(latest.getOpenPrice()) < 0
 2194:                 && closeBelowPrevious
 2195:                 && bearishBodyAtr >= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MIN_BEARISH_BODY_ATR
 2196:                 && closeLocation <= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MAX_CLOSE_LOCATION_FOR_BEARISH_REJECTION;
 2197:         boolean exhaustedPop = closeBelowPrevious
 2198:                 && recentHighFadeAtr >= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MIN_RECENT_HIGH_FADE_ATR
 2199:                 && closeLocation <= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MAX_CLOSE_LOCATION_FOR_EXHAUSTED_POP;
 2200: 
 2201:         String summary = "closeLocation=" + format(closeLocation * 100d) + "%"
 2202:                 + ", bearishBody=" + format(bearishBodyAtr) + " ATR"
 2203:                 + ", fadeFromRecentHigh=" + format(recentHighFadeAtr) + " ATR"
 2204:                 + ", closeVsPrevious=" + (closeBelowPrevious ? "LOWER" : "NOT_LOWER");
 2205: 
 2206:         if (bearishRejection || exhaustedPop) {
 2207:             return ImmediatePriceAction.rejected(summary
 2208:                     + (bearishRejection ? ", bearish rejection" : "")
 2209:                     + (exhaustedPop ? ", exhausted-pop fade" : ""));
 2210:         }
 2211:         return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive(summary);
 2212:     }

 2221:     private HardRiskBlock nonAtrHardRiskBlock(TradeSignal signal) {
 2222:         if (!signal.isStrategyEntryAllowed()) {
 2223:             return HardRiskBlock.block("STRATEGY_ENTRY_BLOCKED", "Selected market strategy does not allow entry.");
 2224:         }
 2225:         if (!signal.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()) {
 2226:             return HardRiskBlock.block("BTC_CONTEXT_BLOCKED", "BTC context issued an entry veto.");
 2227:         }
 2228:         if (!signal.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()) {
 2229:             return HardRiskBlock.block("DERIVATIVES_BLOCKED", "Funding/open-interest positioning issued an entry veto.");
 2230:         }
 2231:         // With wall-lifecycle intelligence, TARGET_BLOCKED should only remain false-entry-allowed
 2232:         // when the wall is genuinely strong/relevant. Weakening walls no longer reach this hard veto.
 2233:         if (!signal.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
 2234:                 && signal.getLiquidityStatus() == LiquidityContextStatus.TARGET_BLOCKED) {
 2235:             return HardRiskBlock.block("STRONG_TARGET_WALL",
 2236:                     "A strong nearby target-side wall is still active; execution remains blocked until fresh order-book evidence changes.");
 2237:         }
 2238:         return HardRiskBlock.none();
 2239:     }

 2241:     private Evidence evidence(TradeSignal current) {
 2242:         // FIX-122: initial-entry scope only; additions keep their original evidence.
 2243:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) stopLossEvidence.requireCurrent(current);
 2244:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);
 2245:         List<TradeSignal> recent = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt());
 2246: 
 2247:         int buy = 0, watch = 0, neutral = 0, bearish = 0, evidenceScore = 0;
 2248:         int scoreTotal = 0, confidenceTotal = 0, qualityCount = 0;
 2249:         int health = OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_START;
 2250:         Instant lastBearishAt = null;
 2251:         Instant oldestObservedAt = null;
 2252:         List<Long> signalIds = new ArrayList<>();
 2253:         List<TradeSignal> observedSequence = new ArrayList<>();
 2254: 
 2255:         for (TradeSignal signal : recent) {
 2256:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
 2257:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;
 2258:             if (stopLossEvidence != null && !stopLossEvidence.eligible(signal, "EVIDENCE")) continue;
 2259: 
 2260:             if (oldestObservedAt == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(oldestObservedAt)) {
 2261:                 oldestObservedAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
 2262:             }
 2263: 
 2264:             observedSequence.add(signal);
 2265: 
 2266:             SignalDecision decision = signal.getDecision();
 2267:             SignalDecision original = signal.getOriginalDecision();
 2268:             SignalDecision effective = strongerDecision(decision, original);
 2269: 
 2270:             if (effective == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
 2271:                 bearish++;
 2272:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_STRONG_SELL, signal);
 2273:                 if (lastBearishAt == null) lastBearishAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
 2274:                 break; // a strong bearish 1m event is still a true memory boundary
 2275:             }
 2276: 
 2277:             if (effective == SignalDecision.SELL) {
 2278:                 bearish++;
 2279:                 evidenceScore -= WEAK_SELL_EVIDENCE_PENALTY;

  788:     @Transactional
  789:     public void markExecuted(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision decision) {
  790:         if (signal == null || decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return;
  791:         currentOpportunity(signal.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
  792:                 .ifPresent(opportunity -> {
  793:                     boolean stillBuilding = "SCOUT_ENTRY".equals(decision.source())
  794:                             || "PRESSURE_PROBE_ENTRY".equals(decision.source())
  795:                             || "CONFIRMATION_ADD".equals(decision.source());
  796:                     opportunity.setStatus(stillBuilding ? "BUILDING" : "EXECUTED");
  797:                     opportunity.setExecutionSource(decision.source());
  798:                     opportunity.setRecommendedPositionPercent(decision.positionPercent());
  799:                     opportunity.setDecisionCode(decision.code());
  800:                     opportunity.setDecisionExplanation(decision.explanation());
  801:                     opportunity.setLatestSignal(signal);
  802:                     opportunity.setLastEvidenceAt(signal.getGeneratedAt());
  803:                     if (!stillBuilding) {
  804:                         opportunity.setExecutedAt(Instant.now());
  805:                     }
  806:                     opportunity.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
  807:                     saveOpportunityEntity(opportunity);
  808:                 });
  809:     }

  844:     /**
  845:      * Entry quality is deliberately independent from signal quality. A market can become
  846:      * more certain while simultaneously becoming a worse price to enter.
  847:      */
  848:     public EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current) {
  849:         return assessEntryQuality(current, current == null ? null : current.getAtrAtSignal(),
  850:                 current == null ? null : current.getLatestPrice());
  851:     }
  852: 
  853:     /**
  854:      * FIX-056: re-score entry quality against the actual execution-time market price.
  855:      * The immutable signal price remains decision evidence only.
  856:      */
  857:     public EntryQuality assessEntryQualityAtPrice(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
  858:         return assessEntryQuality(current, current == null ? null : current.getAtrAtSignal(), executionPrice);
  859:     }
  860: 
  861:     private EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal authoritativeAtr) {
  862:         return assessEntryQuality(current, authoritativeAtr, current == null ? null : current.getLatestPrice());
  863:     }
  864: 
  865:     private EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal authoritativeAtr, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
  866:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || executionPrice == null
  867:                 || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
  868:             return new EntryQuality(0, "UNKNOWN", 0d, 0d, 0d, 0L, false, null, 0, 0d, false, null);
  869:         }
  870: 
  871:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);
  872:         List<TradeSignal> recent = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt());
  873: 
  874:         BigDecimal reference = executionPrice;
  875:         BigDecimal recentHigh = executionPrice;
  876:         Instant recentHighAt = current.getGeneratedAt();
  877:         for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
  878:             if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
  879:             if (s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;
  880:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
  881:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(reference) < 0) {
  882:                 reference = s.getLatestPrice();
  883:             }
  884:             // FIX-091 / Fix 7: mirror the existing rolling-low memory with a rolling high.
  885:             // We intentionally use already-persisted signal prices so Replay and Production
  886:             // observe the same pre-wallet evidence and no new market-data dependency is added.
  887:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
  888:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) > 0) {
  889:                 recentHigh = s.getLatestPrice();
  890:                 recentHighAt = s.getGeneratedAt();
  891:             }
  892:         }
  893: 
  894:         // FIX-055 + FIX-105: keep the opportunity anchor/best floor only while the opportunity
  895:         // is fresh. currentOpportunity() is Replay-aware, and age is measured exclusively from
  896:         // persisted/replayed candle-time evidence (startedAt -> signal.generatedAt), never wall-clock
  897:         // time. Therefore Production and Replay execute this exact same branch deterministically.
  898:         java.util.Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> activeOpportunity = currentOpportunity(
  899:                 current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"));
  900:         String chaseReferenceSource = "ROLLING_30M";
  901:         if (activeOpportunity.isPresent()) {
  902:             ExecutionOpportunity opp = activeOpportunity.get();
  903:             boolean anchorStillFresh = false;
  904:             long opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = 0L;
  905: 
  906:             if (opp.getStartedAt() != null) {
  907:                 Duration opportunityAge = Duration.between(opp.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt());
  908:                 opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = Math.max(0L, opportunityAge.toMinutes());
  909:                 // FIX-105 boundary is inclusive: exactly 120 minutes keeps FIX-055 protection;
  910:                 // the first instant beyond two hours returns authority to the rolling 30m reference.
  911:                 anchorStillFresh = !opportunityAge.isNegative()
  912:                         && opportunityAge.compareTo(ANCHOR_MAX_AGE) <= 0;
  913:             }
  914: 
  915:             if (anchorStillFresh) {
  916:                 BigDecimal anchor = opp.getAnchorEntryPrice();
  917:                 BigDecimal best = opp.getBestEntryPrice();
  918:                 if (anchor != null && anchor.signum() > 0 && anchor.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
  919:                     reference = anchor;
  920:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_ANCHOR";
  921:                 }
  922:                 if (best != null && best.signum() > 0 && best.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
  923:                     reference = best;
  924:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_BEST";
  925:                 }
  926:             }
  927: 
  928:             // FIX-105 diagnostics are deliberately log-only in v1: no schema expansion is required
  929:             // to prove which reference won in Production or Replay. Persisted decision/opportunity
  930:             // evidence remains unchanged.
  931:             log.debug("FIX-105 chase reference symbol={} source={} reference={} opportunityAgeMinutes={} anchorFresh={}",
  932:                     current.getSymbol(), chaseReferenceSource, reference, opportunityAgeMinutesForReference, anchorStillFresh);
  933:         }
  934: 
  935:         double expansionPercent = executionPrice.subtract(reference)
  936:                 .divide(reference, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
  937:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();
  938: 
  939:         double atrExtension = 0d;
  940:         BigDecimal atrForExtension = authoritativeAtr != null && authoritativeAtr.signum() > 0
  941:                 ? authoritativeAtr
  942:                 : current.getAtrAtSignal();
  943:         if (atrForExtension != null && atrForExtension.signum() > 0) {
  944:             atrExtension = executionPrice.subtract(reference).max(BigDecimal.ZERO)
  945:                     .divide(atrForExtension, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
  946:         }
  947: 
  948:         long ageMinutes = activeOpportunity
  949:                 .map(o -> o.getStartedAt() == null ? 0L
  950:                         : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(o.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt()).toMinutes()))
  951:                 .orElse(0L);
  952: 
  953:         double rr = currentRewardRisk(current, executionPrice);
  954: 
  955:         // FIX-091 / Fix 7: a prior high is considered a recent rejection only when price
  956:         // subsequently backed away and the current entry is returning close to that same zone.
  957:         // v1 is deliberately conservative: this only reduces Entry Quality; it never hard-vetoes.
  958:         int rejectionCount = 0;
  959:         double distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = 0d;
  960:         BigDecimal recentRejectedHigh = null;
  961:         if (recentHigh != null && recentHigh.signum() > 0 && recentHighAt != null
  962:                 && recentHighAt.isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())) {
  963:             for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
  964:                 if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(recentHighAt)
  965:                         || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt()) || s.getLatestPrice() == null) continue;
  966:                 if (s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) < 0) rejectionCount++;
  967:             }
  968:             distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = recentHigh.subtract(executionPrice).abs()
  969:                     .divide(recentHigh, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
  970:                     .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();
  971:             if (rejectionCount >= 2 && distanceFromRejectedHighPercent <= 0.50d) {
  972:                 recentRejectedHigh = recentHigh;
  973:             }
  974:         }
  975: 
  976:         int score = 100;
  977: 
  978:         if (expansionPercent > 8d) score -= 50;
  979:         else if (expansionPercent > 5d) score -= 35;
  980:         else if (expansionPercent > 3d) score -= 22;
  981:         else if (expansionPercent > 1.5d) score -= 10;
  982: 
  983:         if (atrExtension > 6d) score -= 30;
  984:         else if (atrExtension > 4d) score -= 20;
  985:         else if (atrExtension > 2.5d) score -= 10;
  986: 
  987:         if (ageMinutes > 60) score -= 15;
  988:         else if (ageMinutes > 40) score -= 10;
  989:         else if (ageMinutes > 20) score -= 5;
  990: 
  991:         if (rr <= 0d || rr < 1d) score -= 30;
  992:         else if (rr < 1.25d) score -= 15;
  993:         else if (rr >= 2d) score += 5;
  994: 
  995:         if (recentRejectedHigh != null) {
  996:             // FIX-091 / Fix 7 v1 is penalty/size-reduction only. A rejection penalty must not
  997:             // by itself push an otherwise non-chase entry below the hard CHASE_ENTRY cutoff.
  998:             int beforeRejectionPenalty = score;
  999:             score -= RECENT_REJECTION_ENTRY_QUALITY_PENALTY;
 1000:             if (beforeRejectionPenalty >= CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF) {
 1001:                 score = Math.max(CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF, score);
 1002:             }
 1003:         }
 1004: 
 1005:         if ("HIGH_VOLATILITY".equals(String.valueOf(current.getMarketRegime()))) score -= 10;
 1006: 
 1007:         // FIX-055: STOP_EXPOSED remains a soft warning (TARGET_BLOCKED is still the
 1008:         // hard liquidity veto), but an exposed stop must reduce entry quality and
 1009:         // therefore position size or block a chase through the existing quality guard.
 1010:         if (current.getLiquidityStatus() == LiquidityContextStatus.STOP_EXPOSED) {
 1011:             score -= STOP_EXPOSED_ENTRY_QUALITY_PENALTY;
 1012:         }
 1013: 
 1014:         if ("BREAKOUT".equals(String.valueOf(current.getMarketRegime()))
 1015:                 && expansionPercent <= 3d && atrExtension <= 2.5d) score += 5;
 1016: 
 1017:         score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
 1018:         String classification = score >= 85 ? "EXCELLENT_ENTRY"
 1019:                 : score >= 70 ? "GOOD_ENTRY"
 1020:                 : score >= 55 ? "ACCEPTABLE_ENTRY"
 1021:                 : score >= CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF ? "LATE_ENTRY"
 1022:                 : "CHASE_ENTRY";
 1023: 
 1024:         boolean moderateBtcConflict = current.getBtcContextStatus() == com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT;
 1025:         boolean transitionProbe = "TRANSITION_PROBE".equals(current.getEntryAuthority());
 1026:         Integer authorityCap = current.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent();
 1027:         return new EntryQuality(score, classification, expansionPercent, atrExtension, rr, ageMinutes,
 1028:                 moderateBtcConflict, recentRejectedHigh, rejectionCount, distanceFromRejectedHighPercent,
 1029:                 transitionProbe, authorityCap);
 1030:     }

 1885:     private ExecutionDecision pressureProbeDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1886:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return null;
 1887: 
 1888:         // Preserve normal BUY authority. A bullish final decision belongs to the normal
 1889:         // path and must never fall back to this probe if normal validation rejects it.
 1890:         if (isBullish(current.getDecision()) || isBearish(current.getDecision())) return null;
 1891:         if (current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.WATCH && current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) return null;
 1892: 
 1893:         if (current.getTotalScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_SCORE
 1894:                 || current.getConfidenceScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_CONFIDENCE
 1895:                 || current.getTrendScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_TREND
 1896:                 || current.getVolumeScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_VOLUME
 1897:                 || current.getMomentumScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_MOMENTUM) return null;
 1898: 
 1899:         // These are existing production safety authorities; the pressure path cannot
 1900:         // manufacture permission when another context/risk layer already vetoed entry.
 1901:         if (!current.isFinalEntryAllowed()
 1902:                 || !current.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
 1903:                 || !current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
 1904:                 || !current.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
 1905:                 || !current.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
 1906:                 || !current.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1907: 
 1908:         PressureReadinessService.Result pressure = pressureReadinessService.evaluate(current);
 1909:         if (pressure == null || !pressure.probeReady()) return null;
 1910: 
 1911:         TradeSignal latestFive = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1912:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1913:         if (latestFive == null || oneHour == null) return null;
 1914: 
 1915:         SignalDecision latestFiveDecision = strongerDecision(latestFive.getDecision(), latestFive.getOriginalDecision());
 1916:         SignalDecision oneHourDecision = strongerDecision(oneHour.getDecision(), oneHour.getOriginalDecision());
 1917: 
 1918:         // A fresh 5m STRONG_SELL is too hostile even for a probe. A plain 5m SELL can
 1919:         // represent the retest only when a recent 5m WATCH/BUY BREAKOUT already proved
 1920:         // the first bullish attempt. This is the SOL sequence: setup -> retest -> rebuild.
 1921:         if (latestFiveDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) return null;
 1922: 
 1923:         Instant setupCutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(PRESSURE_RECENT_5M_SETUP_LOOKBACK);
 1924:         TradeSignal recentFiveMinuteSetup = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1925:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
 1926:                 .filter(s -> !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1927:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(setupCutoff))
 1928:                 .filter(s -> {
 1929:                     SignalDecision d = strongerDecision(s.getDecision(), s.getOriginalDecision());
 1930:                     return d == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(d);
 1931:                 })
 1932:                 .filter(s -> s.getSelectedStrategy() == TradingStrategy.BREAKOUT)
 1933:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_5M_SETUP_SCORE)
 1934:                 .filter(s -> s.getVolumeScore() >= PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_5M_SETUP_VOLUME)
 1935:                 .filter(s -> s.getMomentumScore() >= PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_5M_SETUP_MOMENTUM)
 1936:                 .findFirst()
 1937:                 .orElse(null);
 1938:         if (recentFiveMinuteSetup == null) return null;
 1939: 
 1940:         // The probe may coexist with a bearish 1h because it is explicitly counter-trend
 1941:         // discovery risk, not normal confirmation. We still require a fresh 1h object so
 1942:         // the conflict is known/auditable rather than silently treated as unavailable.
 1943:         if (oneHourDecision == null) return null;
 1944: 
 1945:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1946:                 "PRESSURE_PROBE_ENTRY",
 1947:                 "ABSORPTION_RETEST_REBUILD_PROBE",
 1948:                 PRESSURE_PROBE_POSITION_PERCENT,
 1949:                 "Small pressure probe approved without changing the normal BUY path. "
 1950:                         + pressure.explanation()
 1951:                         + " Current 1m=" + current.getDecision() + " " + current.getTotalScore()
 1952:                         + " (trend=" + current.getTrendScore() + ", volume=" + current.getVolumeScore()
 1953:                         + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore() + "), latest 5m="
 1954:                         + latestFiveDecision + " " + latestFive.getTotalScore()
 1955:                         + ", prior 5m setup #" + recentFiveMinuteSetup.getId() + "="
 1956:                         + strongerDecision(recentFiveMinuteSetup.getDecision(), recentFiveMinuteSetup.getOriginalDecision())
 1957:                         + " " + recentFiveMinuteSetup.getTotalScore() + " " + recentFiveMinuteSetup.getSelectedStrategy()
 1958:                         + ", 1h=" + oneHourDecision + " " + oneHour.getTotalScore()
 1959:                         + ". Exposure is capped at " + PRESSURE_PROBE_POSITION_PERCENT
 1960:                         + "% until the existing progressive/normal confirmation path adds or exits.",
 1961:                 e);
 1962:     }

 1964:     private ExecutionDecision accumulatedDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1965:         if (!isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current)) {
 1966:             return ExecutionDecision.observe("NO_BULLISH_EVIDENCE",
 1967:                     "Current 1m signal does not add BUY/WATCH evidence.", e);
 1968:         }
 1969:         ImmediatePriceAction priceAction = immediatePriceAction(current);
 1970:         if (!priceAction.supportive()) {
 1971:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1972:                     "IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_REVALIDATION_REQUIRED",
 1973:                     "Accumulated evidence remains valid, but the latest fully closed 1m candle no longer confirms an "
 1974:                             + "immediate entry (" + priceAction.summary() + "). The opportunity stays alive and must "
 1975:                             + "wait for fresh price support; WEAK_UPTREND and ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE are not reclassified.",
 1976:                     e);
 1977:         }
 1978:         if (e.fiveMinute() == null || e.oneHour() == null) {
 1979:             return ExecutionDecision.building("MISSING_CONTEXT",
 1980:                     "Opportunity is building, but fresh 5m/1h context is not yet available.", e);
 1981:         }
 1982:         if (isBearish(e.fiveMinute()) || isBearish(e.oneHour())) {
 1983:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("HIGHER_TIMEFRAME_BEARISH",
 1984:                     "Opportunity cancelled because 5m or 1h context is bearish.", e);
 1985:         }
 1986:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < 40) {
 1987:             return ExecutionDecision.building("OPPORTUNITY_RECOVERING",
 1988:                     "Bullish evidence is returning, but opportunity health is only "
 1989:                             + e.opportunityHealth() + "/100 after recent bearish/aging penalties.", e);
 1990:         }
 1991:         if (e.evidenceScore() < MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE) {
 1992:             return ExecutionDecision.building("EVIDENCE_BUILDING",
 1993:                     "Bullish evidence is accumulating: score " + e.evidenceScore() + "/" + MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE
 1994:                             + " from " + e.buyCount() + " BUY and " + e.watchCount() + " WATCH observations.", e);
 1995:         }
 1996:         if (e.buyCount() == 0 && e.watchCount() < 5) {
 1997:             return ExecutionDecision.building("WATCH_ONLY_BUILDING",
 1998:                     "WATCH evidence is persistent but has not yet produced a BUY signal; continuing to observe.", e);
 1999:         }
 2000: 
 2001:         // WATCH-only accumulation is intentionally allowed to keep building across symbols,
 2002:         // but old WATCH observations must not manufacture a fresh BUY when the current
 2003:         // higher-timeframe context is only 1h WATCH + 5m NEUTRAL. In that state there is
 2004:         // no fresh setup confirmation: keep the opportunity memory, but wait for either a
 2005:         // 5m WATCH/BUY or a genuine 1h BUY before committing capital. Existing routes with
 2006:         // real BUY evidence are unchanged, as are HTF_TRANSITION / IMMEDIATE_VALIDATION.
 2007:         if (e.buyCount() == 0
 2008:                 && !isBullish(e.oneHour())
 2009:                 && e.fiveMinute() != SignalDecision.WATCH
 2010:                 && !isBullish(e.fiveMinute())) {
 2011:             return ExecutionDecision.building("WATCH_ONLY_NEEDS_FRESH_CONFIRMATION",
 2012:                     "Accumulated WATCH evidence remains valid, but there is still no BUY observation and fresh "
 2013:                             + "higher-timeframe confirmation is missing (5m=" + e.fiveMinute()
 2014:                             + ", 1h=" + e.oneHour() + "). Wait for 5m WATCH/BUY or 1h BUY before entry.", e);
 2015:         }
 2016:         if (e.averageScore() < 65 || e.averageConfidence() < 65) {
 2017:             return ExecutionDecision.building("EVIDENCE_QUALITY_LOW",
 2018:                     "Evidence persists, but average quality is still low: score=" + e.averageScore()
 2019:                             + ", confidence=" + e.averageConfidence() + ".", e);
 2020:         }
 2021: 
 2022:         // FIX-091 / Fix 8: accumulated evidence is also forbidden from entering after the
 2023:         // current setup has materially deteriorated from the recent opportunity peak. This
 2024:         // directly protects the UNI/PEPE pattern where a strong BUY peak was followed by a
 2025:         // weaker WATCH that later executed only because old evidence remained in memory.
 2026:         EvidenceDeterioration deterioration = assessEvidenceDeterioration(current);
 2027:         if (deterioration.material()) {
 2028:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 2029:                     "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE_DETERIORATED",
 2030:                     deterioration.explanation(),
 2031:                     e);
 2032:         }
 2033: 
 2034:         // FIX-021 / BICOUSDT + ETHUSDT:
 2035:         // Accumulated evidence is memory, not a separate execution authority. It must obey
 2036:         // the SAME configured 5m/1h profile that a normal direct BUY would face. This closes
 2037:         // the contradiction where BALANCED rejected 5m WATCH + 1h NEUTRAL for a fresh BUY,
 2038:         // but ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE later entered with that same or weaker HTF state.
 2039:         TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult authority = validationService.validateBuyContext(current);
 2040:         if (!authority.allowed()) {
 2041:             return ExecutionDecision.building("ACCUMULATED_AUTHORITY_WAIT",

 2043:                             + authority.code() + " - " + authority.explanation(), e);
 2044:         }
 2045:

 2047:         if (e.buyCount() >= 2) percent += 10;
 2048:         if (e.fiveMinute() == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 5;
 2049:         if (isBullish(e.fiveMinute())) percent += 15;
 2050:         if (e.oneHour() == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 5;
 2051:         if (isBullish(e.oneHour())) percent += 10;
 2052:         // Never size accumulated evidence above the authority granted by the configured
 2053:         // direct-BUY profile. Historical evidence may reduce uncertainty, not increase HTF authority.
 2054:         percent = Math.min(Math.min(75, percent), authority.positionPercent());
 2055: 
 2056:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 2057:                 "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE",
 2058:                 "OPPORTUNITY_CONFIRMED",
 2059:                 percent,
 2060:                 "Execution Intelligence approved the current signal from accumulated fresh evidence: "
 2061:                         + e.buyCount() + " BUY, " + e.watchCount() + " WATCH, evidence score=" + e.evidenceScore()
 2062:                         + ", average score=" + e.averageScore() + ", average confidence=" + e.averageConfidence()
 2063:                         + ", 5m=" + e.fiveMinute() + ", 1h=" + e.oneHour()
 2064:                         + ", HTF authority=" + authority.code()
 2065:                         + ". No second trade signal was generated.",
 2066:                 e
 2067:         );
 2068:     }

  844:     /**
  845:      * Entry quality is deliberately independent from signal quality. A market can become
  846:      * more certain while simultaneously becoming a worse price to enter.

  865:     private EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal authoritativeAtr, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
  866:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || executionPrice == null
  867:                 || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
  868:             return new EntryQuality(0, "UNKNOWN", 0d, 0d, 0d, 0L, false, null, 0, 0d, false, null);
  869:         }
  870: 
  871:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);
  872:         List<TradeSignal> recent = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt());
  873: 
  874:         BigDecimal reference = executionPrice;
  875:         BigDecimal recentHigh = executionPrice;
  876:         Instant recentHighAt = current.getGeneratedAt();
  877:         for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
  878:             if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
  879:             if (s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;
  880:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
  881:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(reference) < 0) {
  882:                 reference = s.getLatestPrice();
  883:             }
  884:             // FIX-091 / Fix 7: mirror the existing rolling-low memory with a rolling high.
  885:             // We intentionally use already-persisted signal prices so Replay and Production
  886:             // observe the same pre-wallet evidence and no new market-data dependency is added.
  887:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
  888:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) > 0) {
  889:                 recentHigh = s.getLatestPrice();
  890:                 recentHighAt = s.getGeneratedAt();
  891:             }
  892:         }
  893: 
  894:         // FIX-055 + FIX-105: keep the opportunity anchor/best floor only while the opportunity
  895:         // is fresh. currentOpportunity() is Replay-aware, and age is measured exclusively from
  896:         // persisted/replayed candle-time evidence (startedAt -> signal.generatedAt), never wall-clock
  897:         // time. Therefore Production and Replay execute this exact same branch deterministically.
  898:         java.util.Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> activeOpportunity = currentOpportunity(
  899:                 current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"));
  900:         String chaseReferenceSource = "ROLLING_30M";
  901:         if (activeOpportunity.isPresent()) {
  902:             ExecutionOpportunity opp = activeOpportunity.get();
  903:             boolean anchorStillFresh = false;
  904:             long opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = 0L;
  905: 
  906:             if (opp.getStartedAt() != null) {
  907:                 Duration opportunityAge = Duration.between(opp.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt());
  908:                 opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = Math.max(0L, opportunityAge.toMinutes());
  909:                 // FIX-105 boundary is inclusive: exactly 120 minutes keeps FIX-055 protection;
  910:                 // the first instant beyond two hours returns authority to the rolling 30m reference.
  911:                 anchorStillFresh = !opportunityAge.isNegative()
  912:                         && opportunityAge.compareTo(ANCHOR_MAX_AGE) <= 0;
  913:             }
  914: 
  915:             if (anchorStillFresh) {
  916:                 BigDecimal anchor = opp.getAnchorEntryPrice();
  917:                 BigDecimal best = opp.getBestEntryPrice();
  918:                 if (anchor != null && anchor.signum() > 0 && anchor.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
  919:                     reference = anchor;
  920:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_ANCHOR";
  921:                 }
  922:                 if (best != null && best.signum() > 0 && best.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
  923:                     reference = best;
  924:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_BEST";
  925:                 }
  926:             }
  927: 
  928:             // FIX-105 diagnostics are deliberately log-only in v1: no schema expansion is required
  929:             // to prove which reference won in Production or Replay. Persisted decision/opportunity
  930:             // evidence remains unchanged.
  931:             log.debug("FIX-105 chase reference symbol={} source={} reference={} opportunityAgeMinutes={} anchorFresh={}",
  932:                     current.getSymbol(), chaseReferenceSource, reference, opportunityAgeMinutesForReference, anchorStillFresh);
  933:         }
  934: 
  935:         double expansionPercent = executionPrice.subtract(reference)
  936:                 .divide(reference, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
  937:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();
  938: 
  939:         double atrExtension = 0d;
  940:         BigDecimal atrForExtension = authoritativeAtr != null && authoritativeAtr.signum() > 0
  941:                 ? authoritativeAtr
  942:                 : current.getAtrAtSignal();
  943:         if (atrForExtension != null && atrForExtension.signum() > 0) {
  944:             atrExtension = executionPrice.subtract(reference).max(BigDecimal.ZERO)
  945:                     .divide(atrForExtension, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
  946:         }
  947: 
  948:         long ageMinutes = activeOpportunity
  949:                 .map(o -> o.getStartedAt() == null ? 0L
  950:                         : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(o.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt()).toMinutes()))
  951:                 .orElse(0L);
  952: 
  953:         double rr = currentRewardRisk(current, executionPrice);
  954: 
  955:         // FIX-091 / Fix 7: a prior high is considered a recent rejection only when price
  956:         // subsequently backed away and the current entry is returning close to that same zone.
  957:         // v1 is deliberately conservative: this only reduces Entry Quality; it never hard-vetoes.
  958:         int rejectionCount = 0;
  959:         double distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = 0d;
  960:         BigDecimal recentRejectedHigh = null;
  961:         if (recentHigh != null && recentHigh.signum() > 0 && recentHighAt != null
  962:                 && recentHighAt.isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())) {
  963:             for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
  964:                 if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(recentHighAt)
  965:                         || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt()) || s.getLatestPrice() == null) continue;
  966:                 if (s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) < 0) rejectionCount++;
  967:             }
  968:             distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = recentHigh.subtract(executionPrice).abs()
  969:                     .divide(recentHigh, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
  970:                     .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();
  971:             if (rejectionCount >= 2 && distanceFromRejectedHighPercent <= 0.50d) {
  972:                 recentRejectedHigh = recentHigh;
  973:             }
  974:         }
  975: 
  976:         int score = 100;
  977: 
  978:         if (expansionPercent > 8d) score -= 50;
  979:         else if (expansionPercent > 5d) score -= 35;
  980:         else if (expansionPercent > 3d) score -= 22;
  981:         else if (expansionPercent > 1.5d) score -= 10;
  982: 
  983:         if (atrExtension > 6d) score -= 30;
  984:         else if (atrExtension > 4d) score -= 20;
  985:         else if (atrExtension > 2.5d) score -= 10;
  986: 
  987:         if (ageMinutes > 60) score -= 15;
  988:         else if (ageMinutes > 40) score -= 10;
  989:         else if (ageMinutes > 20) score -= 5;
  990: 
  991:         if (rr <= 0d || rr < 1d) score -= 30;
  992:         else if (rr < 1.25d) score -= 15;
  993:         else if (rr >= 2d) score += 5;
  994: 
  995:         if (recentRejectedHigh != null) {
  996:             // FIX-091 / Fix 7 v1 is penalty/size-reduction only. A rejection penalty must not
  997:             // by itself push an otherwise non-chase entry below the hard CHASE_ENTRY cutoff.
  998:             int beforeRejectionPenalty = score;
  999:             score -= RECENT_REJECTION_ENTRY_QUALITY_PENALTY;
 1000:             if (beforeRejectionPenalty >= CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF) {
 1001:                 score = Math.max(CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF, score);
 1002:             }
 1003:         }
 1004: 
 1005:         if ("HIGH_VOLATILITY".equals(String.valueOf(current.getMarketRegime()))) score -= 10;
 1006: 
 1007:         // FIX-055: STOP_EXPOSED remains a soft warning (TARGET_BLOCKED is still the
 1008:         // hard liquidity veto), but an exposed stop must reduce entry quality and
 1009:         // therefore position size or block a chase through the existing quality guard.
 1010:         if (current.getLiquidityStatus() == LiquidityContextStatus.STOP_EXPOSED) {
 1011:             score -= STOP_EXPOSED_ENTRY_QUALITY_PENALTY;
 1012:         }
 1013: 
 1014:         if ("BREAKOUT".equals(String.valueOf(current.getMarketRegime()))
 1015:                 && expansionPercent <= 3d && atrExtension <= 2.5d) score += 5;
 1016: 
 1017:         score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
 1018:         String classification = score >= 85 ? "EXCELLENT_ENTRY"
 1019:                 : score >= 70 ? "GOOD_ENTRY"
 1020:                 : score >= 55 ? "ACCEPTABLE_ENTRY"
 1021:                 : score >= CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF ? "LATE_ENTRY"
 1022:                 : "CHASE_ENTRY";
 1023: 
 1024:         boolean moderateBtcConflict = current.getBtcContextStatus() == com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT;
 1025:         boolean transitionProbe = "TRANSITION_PROBE".equals(current.getEntryAuthority());
 1026:         Integer authorityCap = current.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent();
 1027:         return new EntryQuality(score, classification, expansionPercent, atrExtension, rr, ageMinutes,
 1028:                 moderateBtcConflict, recentRejectedHigh, rejectionCount, distanceFromRejectedHighPercent,
 1029:                 transitionProbe, authorityCap);
 1030:     }

 1684:     private TradeSignal recentAtrRetracementOrigin(TradeSignal current) {
 1685:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(RETRACEMENT_SETUP_LOOKBACK);
 1686:         return recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1687:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
 1688:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1689:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
 1690:                 .filter(s -> isBullish(s.getOriginalDecision()) || isBullish(s.getDecision()))
 1691:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_SCORE
 1692:                         && s.getTrendScore() >= RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_TREND
 1693:                         && s.getVolumeScore() >= RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_VOLUME)
 1694:                 .filter(s -> !s.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed() && s.isAtrOverextended())
 1695:                 .filter(s -> s.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice() != null && s.getAtrAtSignal() != null)
 1696:                 .filter(s -> s.isStrategyEntryAllowed() && s.isDerivativesEntryAllowed() && s.isLiquidityEntryAllowed())
 1697:                 .filter(s -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(s, "DEFERRED_ORIGIN"))
 1698:                 .max(java.util.Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
 1699:                 .orElse(null);
 1700:     }

 1702:     private boolean isOneHourBearishWorsening(TradeSignal latest, TradeSignal current) {
 1703:         if (latest == null || current == null) return false;
 1704:         List<TradeSignal> hours = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), TREND_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1705:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt() != null && s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(latest.getGeneratedAt()))
 1706:                 .toList();
 1707:         TradeSignal previous = hours.isEmpty() ? null : hours.get(0);
 1708:         if (previous == null) return false;
 1709: 
 1710:         SignalDecision latestEffective = strongerDecision(latest.getDecision(), latest.getOriginalDecision());
 1711:         SignalDecision previousEffective = strongerDecision(previous.getDecision(), previous.getOriginalDecision());
 1712:         boolean severityWorsened = bearishSeverity(latestEffective) > bearishSeverity(previousEffective);
 1713:         return severityWorsened
 1714:                 || latest.getTotalScore() < previous.getTotalScore()
 1715:                 || latest.getTrendScore() < previous.getTrendScore()
 1716:                 || latest.getMomentumScore() < previous.getMomentumScore();
 1717:     }

 1719:     private int bearishSeverity(SignalDecision decision) {

 1721:         if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) return 1;
 1722:         return 0;

 1725:     private ExecutionDecision deferredContinuationDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1726:         if (!isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current)) return null;
 1727:         ImmediatePriceAction priceAction = immediatePriceAction(current);
 1728:         if (!priceAction.supportive()) {
 1729:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1730:                     "IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_REVALIDATION_REQUIRED",
 1731:                     "Deferred continuation stayed technically supportive, but the latest fully closed 1m price action "
 1732:                             + "already rejected the impulse (" + priceAction.summary() + "). Wait for fresh price "
 1733:                             + "support instead of chasing stale derived evidence.",
 1734:                     e);
 1735:         }
 1736:         // Do not require finalEntryAllowed/atrImmediateEntryAllowed here. The aggregate final
 1737:         // entry flag may be false solely because ATR requested a pullback; that is exactly
 1738:         // the condition this continuation route is designed to reassess. All non-ATR hard
 1739:         // vetoes were already enforced before this method is called.
 1740:         if (current.getTotalScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_CURRENT_SCORE
 1741:                 || current.getConfidenceScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_CURRENT_CONFIDENCE) return null;
 1742:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < DEFERRED_MIN_HEALTH
 1743:                 || e.evidenceMomentum() < DEFERRED_MIN_EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM) return null;
 1744:         if (e.fiveMinute() == null || e.oneHour() == null
 1745:                 || isBearish(e.fiveMinute()) || isBearish(e.oneHour())) return null;
 1746: 
 1747:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1748:         if (five == null || !(five.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(five.getDecision()))
 1749:                 || five.getTotalScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_5M_SCORE
 1750:                 || five.getConfidenceScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_5M_CONFIDENCE) return null;
 1751:         if (!(e.oneHour() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(e.oneHour()))) return null;
 1752: 
 1753:         TradeSignal deferredBuy = priorAtrDeferredBuy(current);
 1754:         if (deferredBuy == null) return null;
 1755: 
 1756:         double rewardRisk = currentRewardRisk(current);
 1757:         if (rewardRisk < 1.0d) {
 1758:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1759:                     "CONTINUATION_RISK_REWARD_LOW",
 1760:                     "A prior ATR-deferred BUY exists and continuation is confirmed, but the current reward/risk is only "
 1761:                             + String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.2f", rewardRisk)
 1762:                             + ". The engine will not chase a continuation with less than 1:1 current reward/risk.",
 1763:                     e);
 1764:         }
 1765: 
 1766:         int percent = Math.min(DEFERRED_POSITION_PERCENT,
 1767:                 current.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() > 0
 1768:                         ? current.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent()
 1769:                         : DEFERRED_POSITION_PERCENT);
 1770:         percent = Math.max(20, percent);
 1771: 
 1772:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1773:                 "DEFERRED_CONTINUATION",
 1774:                 "BREAKOUT_CONTINUATION_ENTRY",
 1775:                 percent,
 1776:                 "A prior 1m BUY was deferred only by ATR pullback logic, but the pullback did not arrive. "
 1777:                         + "Fresh continuation is now supported by current 1m evidence, 5m=" + e.fiveMinute()
 1778:                         + " (score=" + five.getTotalScore() + ", confidence=" + five.getConfidenceScore() + ")"
 1779:                         + ", 1h=" + e.oneHour() + ", opportunity health=" + e.opportunityHealth()
 1780:                         + ", evidence momentum=" + e.evidenceMomentum() + ". "
 1781:                         + "Execution uses the current stop/target and a reduced " + percent + "% position.",
 1782:                 e);
 1783:     }

 1785:     private TradeSignal priorAtrDeferredBuy(TradeSignal current) {
 1786:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(DEFERRED_BUY_LOOKBACK);
 1787:         return recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1788:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt() != null
 1789:                         && s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1790:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
 1791:                 .filter(s -> isBullish(s.getDecision()) || isBullish(s.getOriginalDecision()))
 1792:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= properties.minimumBuyScore())
 1793:                 .filter(s -> !s.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed())
 1794:                 .filter(s -> s.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
 1795:                         && s.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
 1796:                         && s.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()
 1797:                         && s.isLiquidityEntryAllowed())
 1798:                 .filter(s -> {
 1799:                     String type = s.getAtrEntryType();
 1800:                     return "PULLBACK_ENTRY".equals(type) || "WAIT_FOR_RETRACEMENT".equals(type);
 1801:                 })
 1802:                 .filter(s -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(s, "DEFERRED_ORIGIN"))
 1803:                 .findFirst()
 1804:                 .orElse(null);
 1805:     }

 1807:     private double currentRewardRisk(TradeSignal signal) {
 1808:         return currentRewardRisk(signal, signal == null ? null : signal.getLatestPrice());
 1809:     }

 1811:     private double currentRewardRisk(TradeSignal signal, BigDecimal marketPrice) {
 1812:         if (signal == null || marketPrice == null || signal.getStopLoss() == null || signal.getTakeProfit() == null) return 0d;
 1813:         java.math.BigDecimal risk = marketPrice.subtract(signal.getStopLoss()).abs();
 1814:         java.math.BigDecimal reward = signal.getTakeProfit().subtract(marketPrice);
 1815:         if (risk.signum() <= 0 || reward.signum() <= 0) return 0d;
 1816:         return reward.divide(risk, 8, java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 1817:     }

 1821:      * confirmation is still lagging. This is intentionally NOT a BUY-promotion method.

 1823:      * <p>Normal direct BUY validation already ran before this method. Therefore a BUY

 1837:      * current signal and it never bypasses a hard risk/context veto.

 1839:     private ExecutionDecision recoveryTransitionDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence evidence) {
 1840:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return null;
 1841:         if (current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.WATCH && current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) return null;
 1842:         if (current.getTotalScore() < 72 || current.getConfidenceScore() < 68

 1851:                 || !current.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()) return null;

 1854:         TradeSignal recentBearish = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1855:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null && s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt()))
 1856:                 .filter(s -> !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(bearishCutoff))
 1857:                 .filter(s -> isBearish(strongerDecision(s.getDecision(), s.getOriginalDecision())))
 1858:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() <= 35)
 1859:                 .findFirst().orElse(null);
 1860:         if (recentBearish == null) return null;

  360:                 Math.min(100, (int) Math.round(atrPercent * executionPercent / 100.0)));
  361:         positionScale = BigDecimal.valueOf(effectivePositionPercent)
  362:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);
  363:         riskAmount = properties.paperAccountBalance()
  364:                 .multiply(properties.riskPerTradePercent(), MC)
  365:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
  366:                 .multiply(positionScale, MC);
  367:         BigDecimal riskPerUnit = freshExecutionPrice.price().subtract(executionSignal.getStopLoss(), MC).abs();
  368: 
  369:         if (riskPerUnit.signum() == 0) {
  370:             throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid stop-loss distance");
  371:         }
  372: 
  373:         BigDecimal riskModelQuantity = riskAmount.divide(riskPerUnit, MC);
  374: 
  375:         final boolean walletExecuted;
  376:         try {
  377:             ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
  378:                     executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price());
  379:             walletExecuted = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price(), freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(), effectivePositionPercent,
  380:                     "Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "] " + executionDecision.explanation()
  381:                             + " DecisionPrice=" + executionSignal.getLatestPrice()
  382:                             + ", executionPrice=" + freshExecutionPrice.price()
  383:                             + ", priceObservedAt=" + freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(),
  384:                     executionDecision.source(),
  385:                     entryQuality.score());
  386:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  387:             log.error("Automatic wallet BUY failed for signal {}: {}", executionSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
  388:             return Optional.empty();
  389:         }
  390:         if (!walletExecuted) {
  391:             log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but wallet controls declined execution; no paper position was created.", executionSignal.getId());
  392:             return Optional.empty();
  393:         }
  394: 
  395:         BigDecimal executedQuantity = walletTradeRepository
  396:                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(executionSignal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
  397:                 .map(com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletTrade::getQuantity)
  398:                 .filter(q -> q != null && q.signum() > 0)
  399:                 .orElse(riskModelQuantity);
  400: 
  401:         executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, executionDecision);
  402:         PaperPosition position = positionRepository.save(PaperPosition.builder()
  403:                 .symbol(symbol)
  404:                 .side(PositionSide.BUY)
  405:                 .status(PositionStatus.OPEN)
  406:                 .quantity(executedQuantity)
  407:                 .entryPrice(freshExecutionPrice.price())
  408:                 .stopLoss(executionSignal.getStopLoss())
  409:                 .takeProfit(executionSignal.getTakeProfit())
  410:                 .signal(executionSignal)
  411:                 .entryReason(executionSignal.getExplanation() + " | Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "]: " + executionDecision.explanation() + " Effective position " + effectivePositionPercent + "%.")
  412:                 .openedAt(Instant.now())
  413:                 .build());
  414:         return Optional.of(position);
  415:     }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No permitted DB result files exist in `db/CODEX`; therefore entry quality for frozen trades `182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137` is presently **unverifiable individually**. No trade can yet be called a chase, high-quality entry, or rule violation.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current entry flow requires a fresh canonical execution price after signal/HTF approval, then revalidates the execution decision at that price. A paper position is created only after wallet BUY execution returns true; its recorded entry price is that fresh execution price. `PaperTradingService.java:340-413`.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current entry-quality scoring is execution-price based, not merely signal-price based. It evaluates: expansion from a rolling/opportunity reference, ATR extension, opportunity age, current reward/risk, return near a rejected high, volatility regime, and stop-exposure liquidity risk. Score bands are:
  - `85+` excellent
  - `70–84` good
  - `55–69` acceptable
  - `50–54` late
  - `<50` chase  
  `ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:845-1029`.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current direct BUY validation requires a bullish 1m execution signal; fresh 5m evidence no older than 20 minutes; and fresh 1h context no older than 3 hours. Profile-specific rules then govern allowed allocation. `TradeExecutionValidationService.java:38-49, 62-112, 300-347`.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current source treats strategy, BTC, derivatives, and strong target-wall conditions as hard entry blocks; a current risk plan—price, stop, and target—is also mandatory. `ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:501-530, 2221-2239`.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `trade_signal` persists the principal contemporaneous signals needed to assess entry: decision/original decision, scores, strategy/context gates, BTC, liquidity, ATR, stop, target, risk/reward, and generated time. `TradeSignal.java:17-411`. `wallet_trade` persists both decision and execution price, execution observation time, quantity, fee, and execution message. `WalletTrade.java:22-62`.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The current code cannot be assumed to be the version active for these historical entries. Even a reconstructed entry-quality score must be reported as a reproduction under evidenced historical code/config only—not inferred from today’s source.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The supplied snapshot timestamps are internally inconsistent: `2026-10-30 12:24:16 UTC` converts to `2026-10-30 15:24:16 KSA`, while the supplied KSA snapshot corresponds to `2026-10-29 17:47:57 UTC`. The frozen IDs remain authoritative; no exact time-window inference should use that clock pair.

- `paper_position` entry signal, entry price, opened time, stop, target, quantity.
- Entry `trade_signal`, plus symbol-matched 1m/5m/1h signals available at or before its generated time.
- Matching executed BUY `wallet_trade`, including decision price, execution price, execution-price observation time, fee, and execution message.
- `execution_opportunity` state around the entry to establish the anchor/best price and opportunity age used by the score.
- Historical configuration/version evidence to establish profile, thresholds, and enabled route.
